**Aktuelni backend runtime incident (2026-10-25; korisnički prijavljeno):** avatar upload završava na `EPERM chmod /usr/app/uploads/private`; source potvrđuje da runtime radi kao UID/GID `10001:10001`, a bind mount prekriva image ownership. Host owner/mode nije provjeren; kodni chmod je privacy guard, zato ga ne uklanjati i ne koristiti `0777`. Upload faila prije file/Upload DB-row write-a. Runbook sada sadrži operator-only, private-subtree permission recovery; agent nije pristupio host volume-u. `HTTP_RATE_LIMIT_REDIS_URL` je izričito neprisutan pa HTTP rate limiting fail-open; konfiguracija se u Coolify mora postaviti kao zaseban least-privilege secret uz documented prefix. Socket connect/disconnect niz nema disconnect reason i sam ne dokazuje backend grešku. Avatari su privatni po defaultu; javni prikaz zahtijeva owner opt-in. Nema source change/deploymenta, host/DB/real-Redis pristupa.<br>

**Novi Redis auth log (2026-10-24):** Novi izlaz sadrži ponovljene greške za publisher i subscriber sa `category=redis-authentication type=Error`, bez allowlisted `code`. Prema sanitizeru, kategorija se javlja kad skrivena poruka sadrži `WRONGPASS`, `NOAUTH`, `authentication required` ili `invalid username-password`; zato je najjači zaključak da Redis odbija ili ne prima ACL auth, ali bez `code` ne razlikujemo pogrešan/URL-neispravno kodiran password, nedostajući auth ili disabled user. Oba klijenta nasljeđuju iste URL credentials, pa su istovremeni neuspjesi konzistentni. Ako je ovo nakon TLS izmjene, vjerovatno se konekcija sada pomjera do Redis AUTH faze; live TLS handshake i dalje nije nezavisno potvrđen. Source nije mijenjan, stvarni Redis nije testiran. Handoff trijaža je pushana kao `ae6ad53`; CI run `40126882655` na tom head-u prošao je sva 4 joba. Vlasnik treba provjeriti (bez dijeljenja tajni) da dedicated ACL user postoji/enabled, da Coolify `SOCKET_IO_REDIS_URL` koristi tačan user/password i da su rezervisani URL znakovi percent-encoded tačno jednom. Nikad ne slati URL s credentials ili lozinku.

**Prethodni Redis startup log (2026-10-24):** Vlasnik je dostavio log u kojem su Prisma migracije završene bez pending migracija, ali backend pada pri `RedisIoAdapter.connectToRedis()` sa `category=tls-handshake type=TypeError` prije Nest listen-a. PostgreSQL i Redis putanje su odvojene; uspješne migracije ne dokazuju Redis TLS. Trenutni sanitizer emituje allowlisted `code` samo kad postoji/prepoznaje ga; ovdje nema `code`, a `tls-handshake` je široka kategorija izvedena iz skrivenog message-a, ne potvrda da je uzrok CA, SAN, port ili sam TLS. `REDIS_TLS`/CA path se validiraju prije kreiranja adaptera; ako isti source radi, adapter log znači da opcije nisu pale na osnovnoj env-validaciji/readable/non-empty CA provjeri, ali ne potvrđuje PEM/trust/hostname. Source nije mijenjan, niti je testirana stvarna Redis veza. Handoff triage update je pushan kao `094f8ba`; CI run `40124632513` na tom head-u prošao je sva 4 joba. Sljedeće tražiti samo sanitizirane vrijednosti `REDIS_TLS`, `REDIS_CA_CERT_PATH` i scheme/hostname/port `SOCKET_IO_REDIS_URL` (bez userinfo), potvrdu da je CA za Redis server i SAN pokriva URL hostname, te eventualne prethodne log linije `client=publisher|subscriber`. Nikad ne tražiti URL s credentialima ili CA sadržaj.

**Redis startup diagnostic follow-up (2026-10-24; targeted tests and CI passed):** Vlasnik je poslao novi stack trace koji završava u `RedisIoAdapter.connectToRedis()` sa generičkim `Socket.IO Redis adapter initial connection failed.`; postojeći catch odbacuje originalni uzrok, pa sam trace ne razlikuje TLS/certifikat, port/protokol, DNS, auth, ACL ili timeout. **PostgreSQL TLS mount/code check (2026-10-24; local checks and CI passed):** Vlasnik potvrđuje da je DB TLS uključen i traži mount `/data/coolify/ssl/coolify-ca.crt:/etc/ssl/certs/coolify-ca.crt:ro`; dodat je u backend Compose, odvojeno od postojećeg Redis CA mounta. Pregled koda potvrđuje da Compose prosljeđuje `DATABASE_URL` bez transformacije, a `PrismaService`, seed i media-storage migration runner daju URL `PrismaPg` adapteru. Zaključani `pg-connection-string` v2.14.0 čita `sslrootcert`, učitava CA fajl, a `sslmode=verify-full` ne isključuje certifikat/hostname provjeru. Lokalni parser eksperiment sa privremenim sintetičkim CA pokazao je da literalni `&` učitava root CA, dok HTML `&amp;` ne formira parametar `sslrootcert`. Ako URL već sadrži `?schema=public`, TLS parametre treba dodati sa `&`, a ne drugim `?`. Env template i architecture docs su ažurirani. Compose/env/docs izmjene su commitovane kao `9a1e09a` (`chore(compose): mount Coolify PostgreSQL CA`) i pushane; Handoff CI head je `fe19791`. Prettier YAML parse, repo hygiene (1.131 path names), Markdown links (560/168) i `git diff --check` prolaze. CI run `40123214517` prošao je sva 4 joba (Step 275). Nema stvarne DB konekcije, host CA pristupa, certifikatske/hostname provjere ili deploymenta.

Web/mobile clients now have app-local manual recovery controllers with Socket.IO Manager reconnection disabled: each retry reuses the current auth token for a DB-backed handshake, server/transport and non-`Unauthorized` connection errors use bounded 1s→2s→4s→5s backoff, and exact `Unauthorized` stops retries/disconnects without clearing auth or logging out. Follow-up also found a separate confirmed React Native multipart defect; its targeted correction is committed/pushed as `648d507`; CI run `39964258790` completed successfully across all four jobs, including the multipart regression. Web body evidence now points to the global interceptor issue described below; the response status/`Content-Type` and post-fix browser verification remain pending. Earlier docs-only CI run `39955204710` for `0f92dfb` has now been checked and completed successfully (Step 225).

**Web avatar follow-up (2026-10-23):** Owner confirms the failing client is web. The avatar POST response contains an avatar path and `isAvatarPublic: true`; the next supplied body is a JSON object exposing `StreamableFile` internals (`options`, `logger`, `stream`) and a 7,614-byte buffer beginning with `RIFF`. If this is the protected avatar GET response, it matches the source defect: global `DecimalToNumberInterceptor` recursively rebuilt the `StreamableFile` object, removing the Nest Express adapter's stream type and causing JSON serialization instead of image bytes. Added an explicit pass-through for `StreamableFile` and `Buffer`, a unit regression, and registered the global interceptor in the synthetic DB-backed HTTP harness so its existing avatar binary/MIME assertions cover the production response path. Commit `aabdd1e` is pushed; CI run `40014198802` passed all four jobs, including the DB-backed avatar binary/MIME assertions with the global interceptor active. Owner did not provide the GET status/`Content-Type`, and no live browser/device/deployment was tested.

**Latest owner report (2026-10-21):** The Redis URL had been `rediss://`; the owner now reports a successful redeploy with backend and frontend running. This is not an agent-side live verification; exact final URL/ACL settings and the earlier failure's root cause remain unconfirmed.

**SEC-04/PRIV-01 current status (2026-10-23):** Owner-approved consent policy and Bosnian copy v1 (`client-media-publication-v1`) are implemented, including consent/revoke gates, private originals, protected derivatives, the hybrid Gallery, self-managed private-by-default avatars, and migration safeguards. The Gallery has at most one auto-publish category (service conflict plus partial unique DB index); public pairs require two distinct currently consented images and expose no client/service/date fields. Admin/owner manual uploads require the non-client attestation and image re-encoding; client photos enter manual categories only via the active-consent picker. This attestation is not automatic person detection. Avatar visibility applies only to the owner's avatar; administrators cannot change another user's avatar. The media migration planner preserves public status only for `PUBLIC` uploads linked to Gallery rows and rooted under `gallery/`; legacy/unlinked files remain private. The runner quarantines unmapped files from the future static public root before DB mapping and retains bytes privately; recovery/retention, backup/restore, rollback and production cutover remain gates. No migration runner or production/shared data was touched.

## Cilj i pravila rada

Kroz kod potvrditi arhitekturu i ponašanje web/API/mobile dijelova, dokumentovati potvrđene bugove/rizike i nedostatke u dizajnu, kontrastu i prevodima, te pripremiti fazni implementacijski plan, provjere i CI. Nalazi moraju imati putanju/linije i jačinu dokaza; heuristike se ne predstavljaju kao potvrđeni runtime bugovi. Ne prikazivati vrijednosti tajni. Ne otvarati PR prema `master`, ne mergeati i ne zatvarati sesiju; korisnik radi ručni merge ako ga odobri. Nakon svakog izvršenog koraka/faze ažurirati ovaj dokument.

## Izvršeni koraci

1. **Početni inventar i dokumentacija:** evidentirani NestJS/Prisma backend, React/Vite frontend, Expo/React Native mobile, Docker konfiguracije, package skripte i postojeći poslovni vodiči. Raniji pregled je brojio 731 tracked izvorni fajl i oko 81K TS/TSX redova. Pronađene su zastarjele/proturječne tvrdnje u `SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md`, `docs/codebase-map/*`, prezentacijama i `.cursor/docs/*`.
2. **Početni statički pregled:** čitani su auth/JWT/RBAC, uploads, users/settings, reservations, daily-log, finance, notifications/events, blog, reviews, gallery, loyalty, analytics i app releases; pregledani su Prisma modeli/migracije te web/mobile routing, i18n i theme tokeni. Audit nije završen; svaki kandidat se provjerava uz okolni controller/service/DTO/schema/test.
3. **Build/test baseline (2026-10-21):** frontend `npm run build` prolazi (Vite upozorava na ~2.003 kB JS chunk); mobile `npx tsc --noEmit` prolazi. Backend testovi su djelimično mogli startati, ali 35/53 suitea ne učitava jer nedostaje generisani Prisma Client. `prisma validate` i `prisma generate --no-engine` blokirani su TLS prekidom pri preuzimanju Prisma enginea; backend build zato nije potvrđen. Backend ESLint je dao 6.418 dijagnostika uz nedostajući Prisma Client, pa rezultat nije valjan baseline. Frontend/mobile `lint` skripte ne rade jer ESLint nije instaliran. Expo provjera verzija je blokirana istom vrstom mrežnog prekida. Docker CLI nije dostupan.
4. **Prisma/dokumentacija provjera:** aktivna Prisma šema, adapter i migration lock su PostgreSQL. U repozitoriju postoji PostgreSQL baseline migracija (`20260822100257_postgresql_baseline`) i naredna migracija (`20260915085100_blog_post_seo`); dio dokumentacije još tvrdi MySQL/MariaDB ili da PostgreSQL migracija tek treba nastati. `.github/` i workflow fajlovi ne postoje, iako jedan tooling dokument navodi deploy workflow.
5. **Sigurnosna higijena — izvršena promjena:** utvrđeno je da su `backend/.env`, `frontend/.env`, `mobile/.env` i jedan Firebase Admin SDK service-account JSON privatne putanje; vrijednosti nisu ispisane. Trenutni worktree prikazuje sva četiri kao obrisana, ali je kasnija `git ls-files` provjera kroz repo-hygiene skriptu potvrdila da su putanje još u indexu, pa ranija tvrdnja da su uklonjene iz indexa nije potvrđena trenutnim snapshotom. U ovoj fazi sadržaj nije pregledan, a fajlovi nisu vraćani, stage-ovani ni mijenjani. Service-account putanja nije korištena u pregledanom kodu. `mobile/google-services.json` je zadržan — to je klijentska Firebase konfiguracija, ne isti tip privatnog service-account ključa. Root `.gitignore` je dopunjen za lokalne env fajlove, service-account/signing materijal i build artefakte; uklonjeno je preširoko pravilo `test` koje je ignorisalo direktorije testova. `git check-ignore` je potvrdio ignore pravila za lokalne env putanje, a `.env.example` ostaje trackable. **Git istorija nije prepisana:** brisanje iz trenutnog worktree-a ne uklanja ranije commitovane vrijednosti.
6. **Sigurnosni kandidati potvrđeni do sada (još nisu kompletno razvrstani):** javni blog list endpoint može vratiti neobjavljene postove; javni endpoint komentara podrazumijeva i neodobrene komentare; javni reviews endpoint podrazumijeva i neodobrene recenzije, a `GET /api/reviews/:id` uključuje cijeli povezani stylist User. `GET /api/users/clients` je dozvoljen i stylist roli, dok list query koristi Prisma `include` bez allowlist `select`, što vraća i osjetljiva User polja (npr. hash/token). Upload delete ruta ima samo JWT guard i ne provjerava vlasništvo/ulogu; upload root se javno servira, uključujući dokumente. Before/after fotke klijenata ulaze u javni endpoint bez vidljivog consent/visibility polja. Postoje raw HTML/SVG render putanje, fail-open enkripcija app secrets, poznata početna lozinka i nedostatak server-side provedbe obavezne promjene lozinke. Svaki nalaz će biti precizno dokumentovan uz tačne putanje/linije, pogođene uloge i ograničenja.
7. **Dodatni ciljani pregled (2026-10-21):** osvježen inventar broji 299 backend TS/JS izvora (~28.9K linija), 263 frontend (~34.4K), 113 `mobile/src` (~15.9K) i 50 Expo route fajlova (~2.1K); stvarni backend moduli su auth, users, salon, services, stylists, reservations, daily-log, finance, loyalty, blog, gallery, reviews, notifications, events, analytics, settings, uploads i app-releases (plus core/common/config/i18n/prisma). Potvrđena je i dodatna klasa širokih Prisma responsea: `IncomeBookRepository.manualEntryInclude` i detaljni finance query uključuju `stylistProfile.user: true` (potencijalno vraća osjetljiva User kolone u finance payloadima); pregledani daily-log client include je, nasuprot tome, eksplicitno ograničen na ime/prezime. `ReviewsService.findOne` ima `stylist.user: true`; route public status i response treba završno citirati uz controller. Loyalty redeem/adjust ima read-then-write sekvence: `redeemPoints` čita saldo pa bez saldo uslova radi atomarni decrement, dok reservation/daily-log marker se ažurira u zasebnom pozivu; konkurentni zahtjevi mogu prekoračiti saldo ili skinuti bodove bez vezanog popusta. `adjustPoints` koristi pročitan saldo pa `set`, pa paralelne izmjene mogu izgubiti balans iako obje zapišu transakciju. Ovo su statički dokazane race putanje; reprodukcija nad PostgreSQL još nije urađena. `ClientDebtsService.repayDebt` također provjerava `OPEN` prije transakcije i unutar transakcije bez uslovnog status updatea kreira debt-payment i prihod; konkurentni pozivi mogu dvostruko knjižiti isti dug. Finance book-open provjera je prije transakcije, pa race s lockom ostaje za test/fix plan. Nije pronađen nijedan `.dockerignore`; lokalni env fajlovi mogu ući u Docker *build context* (finalni backend image stage ih ne kopira eksplicitno, ali build-stage/cache izlaganje ostaje deployment rizik). Stvarni kod counts i naredni kandidat nalazi nisu još prebačeni u finalni report.

8. **Auth, HTTP boundary i public response pass (2026-10-21):** `UsersController` dopušta `STYLIST` na `GET /api/users/clients`; `UsersService.findStrictClients` radi `include` (bez `select`) i bez `take` kada paginacija nije kompletna, dok `User` sadrži password hash i email verification token/expiry. Public reviews route potvrđena je na controlleru: list ne filtrira `isApproved` ako query nije tačno `approved=true`, a javni `GET /api/reviews/:id` vraća `stylist.user: true`. Public blog list tretira odsutan/false `published` kao “svi postovi”; public comments ruta analogno podrazumijeva `approved=false` filter (dakle svi). `findBySlug` je korektno ograničen na objavljene postove i odobrene komentare, ali `GET /api/blog/:id` je samo JWT-zaštićen i čita bez status provjere. Daily-log customer relation koristi allowlist imena, ali finance repository koristi `user:true` u stylist profile include. Auth UI route-guard nije server-side policy: global JWT guard validira aktivnost i osvježava role iz baze, ali ne provjerava `mustChangePassword`/email verified; login/register izdaju token, a registration kod kreira `isEmailVerified=false`. Public verification prima šestocifreni kod na 24h i u kodu nije pronađen throttling/lockout; repozitorij nema `@nestjs/throttler` integraciju. `JWT_SECRET` fallback literal se koristi i u JwtModule i JwtStrategy/token sign; startup provjera prisustva/entropije nije nađena. `SettingsEncryptionService.encrypt` vraća plaintext ako nema validnog base64 32-byte encryption key, umjesto da faila startup/write. `main.ts` reflektuje svaki CORS origin (`origin:true`, `credentials:true`), bez Helmet konfiguracije; proxy/CSP deployment sloj nije potpuno provjeren. Upload kontroler prihvata svaki autentifikovani nalog, ograničava samo veličinu i ne veže deletion za owner/role; SVG se pohranjuje originalno, a `/uploads/*` se servira statički. Ovo povećava XSS/privacy rizik, posebno za finance attachments; stvarni browser exploit/response headeri nisu testirani. `GET /api/gallery/categories/:id` vraća `upload:true`, uključujući metapodatke Upload modela (ne samo javni URL). `POST /api/uploads` ruta ne traži admin rolu.
9. **Pregled kvaliteta/repo packaging (baseline prije izmjena, 2026-10-21):** `backend/package.json` lint komanda je koristila `--fix`; `test:e2e` pokazuje na nepostojeći `backend/test/jest-e2e.json`; frontend/mobile imaju lint skripte bez ESLint dependency/config i nemaju test runner ni test files. Frontend build koristi `tsc -b`; tracked generated output (`*.tsbuildinfo`, `vite.config.js/.d.ts`) postoji; u Git-u su prazni `backend/tsc-errors.txt` i `mobile/tsc-errors.txt` (cleanup kandidat nije izvršen). `npm ci` je prošao za sva tri paketa. Docker builds su tada bili bez `.dockerignore`; Compose backend koristi external `db_net`, nema DB/Redis service/healthcheck, a backend Dockerfile radi migracije pri svakom startu. `mobile/Dockerfile` je placeholder. Codebase/presentation docs su sadržavali zastarjele MySQL/MariaDB, Expo 55, migration-empty i CI/deploy tvrdnje; nalazi su ispravljeni u kasnijem koraku 17.

10. **Business-flow, time, privacy i UI pass (2026-10-21):** važno ispraviti nijansu iz prethodne bilješke: `ReservationSharedService.getClientForReservation` **serverski** odbija klijenta sa `mustChangePassword=true`, bez email/phone ili sa neverified emailom; to važi za redovni booking API. Ne postoji globalni API gate, pa ostale rute i dalje ne provjeravaju taj onboarding policy. `ReservationCreateService.create` upisuje reservation, service items, stylist responses, šalje notifikacije i tek potom obrađuje loyalty, bez jedne transakcije/outbox-a; djelimičan neuspjeh može ostaviti nepotpunu rezervaciju ili već potrošene bodove. Availability endpointi ne validiraju date/range format ni maksimalnu dužinu; date service za svaki dostupni dan radi `getAvailableSlots` kroz `Promise.all`, dok browser klijent zove 60-dnevni raspon. Public before/after filtrira samo COMPLETED/nondeleted SERVICE sa before+after slikama; nema consent/visibility polja u modelu/queryju. Random putanja učita sve eligible IDs i shuffle-uje ih u memoriji; offset je samo clampovan na >=0 bez gornje granice. `DailyLogService.complete` postavi daily-log COMPLETED pa reservation COMPLETED prije KIF sync-a; sync failure vraća grešku, ali retry blokira status check; postoji ručni integrity/resync za admina, pa je recovery moguća, ali nije automatska niti u istoj transakciji. Loyalty earn greška se guta (`catch(() => {})`), bez idempotency unique constrainta za `daily_log:<id>`; redeem/account+marker write nisu transakcijski, a adjust radi read-then-set. `ClientDebtsService.listForClient` dozvoljava bilo kojem STYLIST-u proizvoljan clientId; to je šire od zasebne `stylist/me` liste i zahtijeva policy odluku. Debt repay status/knjiga-open provjere su prije transakcije; unutar tx status se unconditional update-a i kreiraju se debt-payment/ledger rows, pa race može duplirati naplatu. Review create dopušta `reservationId` izostaviti; tada ne provjerava da je review od stvarnog klijenta/usluge (autentičnost recenzije nije enforce-ovana). `getAvailableDates`/`getAvailableSlots` nisu označeni `@Public` u ovom controlleru, nego traže JWT; no rate limiting nije nađen.
11. **Supply-chain/UI statičke provjere (2026-10-21):** `npm audit --omit=dev --json` je na dostupnom registry snapshotu exit 1 za sve tri aplikacije: backend 15 (13 high/critical, od toga 1 critical; 2 moderate), frontend 5 (2 high, 3 moderate), mobile 41 (29 high/critical, od toga 1 critical; 12 moderate). Primjeri/advisory surface: backend `proxy-addr`, `@nestjs/platform-express`, `multer`, `nodemailer`, `sharp`, `undici`, Prisma/MariaDB transitive; frontend/mobile `axios`, `follow-redirects`, React Router; mobile i Expo/Metro/nativewind/react-native toolchain + `shell-quote`. Ovo su lockfile advisories, ne dokaz da je svaki path exploitable u runtime-u; runtime reachability treba odvojeno analizirati. Nije pokrenut `npm audit fix` jer Expo/React Native/Router predložene popravke uključuju major/SDK skokove i mogu biti breaking. Translation leaf parity: frontend bs=290, en=108, 182 bs keys nemaju EN; mobile ima samo bs resource. UI literal/accessibility regex counts su heuristike, ne potpuna a11y provjera. Novi `scripts/check_ui_contrast.py` izračunao je 23 odabrana source-token para; 9 je ispod njihovih pragova: web light primary/background 2.37:1, muted 4.40:1, destructive 3.59:1, ring/background 2.37:1; mobile light gray400/white 1.88:1, gold/white 2.21:1, rose/white 2.73:1, gray400/gray50 1.80:1; mobile dark gray600/black 3.78:1. Ovo nisu rendered UI/browser provjere. Dinamičke admin boje prihvataju validan HEX bez source-time kontrastne provjere. Login nema password-reset UI/rutu u backendu, iako axios interceptor sadrži prepoznate stringove za `/auth/forgot-password` i `/auth/reset-password`. Frontend build/mobile TypeScript prolaze; nema front/mobile automated test suite-a.

12. **Follow-up: public API serializers, event fanout, timezone i finance closure (2026-10-21):** pronađen i direktno potvrđen nalaz **Critical/P0**: `GET /api/reviews/:id` je `@Public()`, a `ReviewsService.findOne` vraća `stylist: { include: { user: true } }`; Prisma default include vraća i scalar `User.passwordHash`, `emailVerificationToken`, expiry, OAuth IDs i ostale account atribute. Javni `GET /api/reviews` vraća review ID-eve i bez `approved=true` uključuje neodobrene zapise, pa ID-jevi nisu praktična zaštita od enumeracije. Ovo je statički dokaz putanje/response projection, nije live HTTP proof; odmah treba ukloniti javni detail response ili koristiti eksplicitni public DTO, a nakon deploya procijeniti incident i prisilnu credential reset/revocation politiku za stiliste. Zasebno, `GET /api/users/clients` dozvoljava STYLIST rolu i `findStrictClients` vraća `User` bez `select` (i bez gornje granice ako paginacija nije kompletna), čime se izlažu klijentski password hash i email-verification token/expiry; paginacija ne uklanja overexposure. Public `GET /api/blog` defaultira na sve postove, a public comments/reviews list defaultiraju na neodobrene; blog list vraća pun sadržaj drafta. Public `GET /api/services`, kategorija/subkategorija i `GET /api/stylists` koriste `activeOnly=false` ako klijent izostavi parametar; stylist public projection eksplicitno sadrži email/telefon. Public UI obično šalje `activeOnly=true` za neke liste, ali javni API default ostaje širi i detail rute ne provode active status.

`SETTINGS_UPDATED` i `SALON_UPDATED` idu kroz `EventsGateway.emitToAll()` (`server.emit`) dok Gateway prihvata anonimne konekcije; postavke šalju key/value za svaki registry item osim samo onog označenog `isSecret`, pa public socket dobija i interne, ne-public setting vrijednosti. Reservation payload eventi su uglavnom usmjereni na user/admin/stylist rooms; `AVAILABILITY_CHANGED` je namjerno broadcast. Vrijednosti tajnih postavki se ne šalju, ali pristup event streamu nije ograničen po setting scope. Nezavisno, public salon REST response vraća cijeli `SalonSettings` model i buduće holiday zapise; tačan privacy policy nije dokumentovan.

`ReservationDateService` koristi `Europe/Sarajevo` konfigurabilni TZ za wall-time conversion, međutim `AvailabilitySlotsService` poredi današnje slotove sa `Date.getHours()/getMinutes()` i lokalnim `toLocalDateOnlyString`, a `ReservationDelayCron`/`ReservationReorderCronService` opet koriste process-local `getHours()`/`getDay()`; Dockerfile ne postavlja `TZ`. Ako server TZ nije salon TZ (čest UTC container), isti-dan availability i cron upozorenja/reorder mogu kasniti ili žuriti za UTC offset. Ovo je statički potvrđena nedosljednost; treba testirati pod `TZ=UTC` i `Europe/Sarajevo`, uključujući DST, prije označavanja produkcijskog uticaja.

Finansijski lock enforcement je provjeren: `ExpensesService.create/update/remove/attachment` čita `book.isClosed` prije write/tx; `IncomeManualEntriesService` poziva `getEditableBookOrThrow` prije transakcije. Konkurentni close-vs-write može proći kroz stale check; potreban je DB-level uslov/lock i PostgreSQL race test. Compose trajno mapira upload folder na host, ali ne definiše DB/Redis, healthcheck ni migration job; backend Docker `CMD` pokreće `prisma migrate deploy` pri svakom startu. Samo `@Cron` schedule je process-local; trenutno nema Redis adapter/queue/distributed lock. Potencijalno dupliciranje poslova pri horizontalnom scale-u nije runtime testirano.

13. **Reservation, upload, messaging i documentation follow-up (2026-10-21):** `ReservationCreateService.create` serverski provjerava eligibility slot i client-day policy, ali zatim obavlja reservation create, service-item createMany, stylist-response createMany, sekvencijalne notifikacije i loyalty redeem kroz odvojene write pozive bez jedne transakcije/outbox-a; greška kasnije u toku može ostaviti booking (ili dio stavki) uz HTTP error, odnosno nepotrošene/već potrošene bodove. U queryju za odabrane servise i slotu nema `isActive=true`, pa deaktivirani servis s poznatim ID-em nije server-side odbijen kod kreiranja; UI ga sakriva, ali API može ostati putanja za booking. Availability `fromDate`/`toDate` se pretvaraju u Date bez formata/opsega provjere; service za svaki dan poziva slot lookup kroz Promise.all bez maksimalnog intervala. Ova check-path je statički potvrđena; konkurentno overbooking ponašanje nije reprodukovano nad PostgreSQL-om.

Public `GET /api/stylists` je `@Public`, default `activeOnly=false`, vraća neaktivne profile i `user.email`; public detail uključuje email + phone. Public services/category/subcategory endpoints također defaultiraju na nefiltrirane inactive redove, a `findOne*` detalji ne enforce-uju active status. Public UI djelimično šalje activeOnly flag, ali kategorija-detail UI zatim dohvaća nefiltrirane nested subcategories/services. `DailyLogImage` model nema consent/visibility polje; public before/after servis vraća direktne javne `/uploads` putanje za dovršene/deletedAt-null SERVICE zapise sa before+after slikama.

`UploadsController` prihvata svaki autentifikovani upload, provjerava samo naziv postojanja/veličinu, `UploadsService` odlučuje po korisničkom MIME headeru i prihvata SVG, PDF, Word i Excel; cijeli root se servira javno preko statičkog prefixa. Time su finance invoice/attachments javno dostupni po URL-u. Delete-by-path ne dobija user context i ne provjerava ownership/attachment references; autentifikovani korisnik koji zna path može obrisati tuđi upload. Frontend inline SVG sanitizacija je regex-based i `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`-based, ne robustan parser/allowlist; public blog sadržaj ima još jedan raw `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` sink. Actual XSS response/renderer nije izvršen.

`NotificationsService.sendToMany` radi recipient-e sekvencijalno; email campaign također dohvaća cijelu publiku i `sendMail` radi jedan-po-jedan u HTTP requestu bez background queue/retry/idempotency. Normalne notifikacije su DB + opcionalni in-app realtime/Expo push/SMTP prema preferencama. Ovo je skalabilnost/reliability rizik, ne dokaz trenutne produkcijske timeout greške. Public email-verification ruta nema pronađen throttling/lockout; backend nema password-reset endpoint dok frontend interceptor još prepoznaje stringove za reset putanje. Frontend čuva bearer JWT u `localStorage`; mobile ga čuva u `expo-secure-store`.

Dodatna dokumentacijska provjera pronašla je stare tvrdnje u `SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md`, `backend/README.md`, `backend/POSTGRES_MIGRATION.md`, codebase maps i prezentacijama. Potvrđeno direktnim path listingom: aktivna `prisma/migrations/` sadrži baseline + SEO migration; odvojeni tracked `prisma/migrations_mysql_backup/` postoji (34 files) i jeste historijski archive, ne aktivna Postgres migration putanja. `backend/README.md` je bio generički Nest starter; `POSTGRES_MIGRATION.md` pogrešno tvrdio da je active migration folder prazan; codebase maps/presentations su imali stale DB/SDK/workflow i email-stub opis. Relevantni docs su sada osvježeni; detaljna prije/poslije tabela nalazi se u `FINDINGS.md`. Dockerfile nakon izmjene koristi samo build-stage dummy URL; runtime DB URL ostaje environment supplied.

## Hitno za vlasnika sistema

Pošto su credential fajlovi bili u Git istoriji, tretirati njihove vjerodajnice kao kompromitovane bez obzira na to što nisu prikazane u ovom chatu. Rotaciju/revokaciju koordinirati kroz secret manager/konzolu, ne u repozitoriju. Posebno: Firebase Admin key opozvati i izdati novi samo ako je potreban; DB credential i JWT secret rotirati; promjenu `APP_SETTINGS_ENCRYPTION_KEY` ne raditi naslijepo — postojeće `enc:v1` vrijednosti treba prvo dešifrovati/re-enkriptovati ili uvesti kontrolisanu dual-key migraciju. Promjena JWT tajne će prekinuti aktivne tokene. Nakon koordinacije razmotriti historijsko uklanjanje tajni pomoću history rewrite-a; to traži dogovor za sve klonove i deploy izvore.

## Owner action items carried forward

Credential rotation/revocation, deployment log review, consent/public profile policy decisions and manual security deployment remain outside repo-only static verification; see `FINDINGS.md` and `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`.

- [Nalazi statičkog audita](FINDINGS.md) — severity, dokazi/putanje, uticaj, preporuke i runtime caveats; uključuje security, privacy, finance, API, accessibility, i18n, dependencies i dokumentacijske nesuglasice.
- [Implementacijski plan](IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md) — faze 0–7, acceptance tests, rollback, redis criteria i owner decisions.

CI opis: [CI.md](CI.md).

66. **SEC-06 source re-check (2026-10-21):** `SettingsService.set` currently sends every setting update through `EventsGateway.emitToAll` with `normalizedValue` except `isSecret` values; it does not inspect registry `scope`. The registry contains numerous `PRIVATE` non-secret values (including email host/username and internal policies). `EventsGateway` permits anonymous sockets and implements `emitToAll` as `server.emit`; frontend/mobile `settings:updated` listeners refresh public content, and the frontend admin settings hook listens to the same event. Source-level leak confirmed; no live Socket.IO probe. Planned narrow fix: broadcast `{key,value}` only for non-secret `PUBLIC` settings; send a key-only notification to the admin room for `PRIVATE` settings so the admin UI refreshes via its guarded HTTP route.

67. **SEC-06 scope-aware settings event mitigation (2026-10-21):** `SettingsService.set` now broadcasts `{key,value}` only for registry entries that are both `PUBLIC` and not secret; private or secret updates send a key-only `SETTINGS_UPDATED` notification to the admin room. Added regression assertions for public normalized values, private admin-only key notifications, and secret-value non-disclosure. This preserves public settings refresh and admin settings refresh; no live Socket.IO/DB test yet. First local targeted Jest attempt could not start because this fresh workspace has no backend `node_modules` (`jest: not found`); dependencies were then installed with the locked package list.

119. **Client recovery implementation scope (2026-10-21):** Before coding, inspected the actual configs/scripts: frontend uses `frontend/tsconfig.json` and Vite, mobile uses Expo `mobile/tsconfig.json`, and neither package has a test runner. The mobile socket is created in `mobile/src/realtime/socket-client.ts`. Plan: disable Socket.IO's built-in reconnect loop to avoid competing retries, add a small dependency-free reconnect controller per app with bounded exponential backoff, retry non-`Unauthorized` connection failures, reauthenticate through the existing token handshake/current DB lookup, and stop the loop on exact backend `Unauthorized`. Add Node built-in regression tests in both packages and CI; do not clear tokens/change logout UX or contact Redis/DB/deployment.

120. **Web/mobile socket recovery implemented (2026-10-21):** Both providers attach an app-local manual recovery controller before opening the socket, and Socket.IO Manager reconnection is disabled to avoid duplicate retry loops. Server/transport disconnects and non-`Unauthorized` `connect_error`s retry at 1s, 2s, 4s, then a 5s cap; successful connections reset the delay. Each attempt uses the active token and re-runs the backend handshake/current DB role check. Exact `Unauthorized` cancels the loop and disconnects locally; no token clearing or logout behavior was added. Web token updates cancel stale timers and reconnect using the new token; mobile creates the socket without auto-connect so its provider installs recovery/state listeners before connecting. Added dependency-free Node regression suites and package scripts in both apps; CI now runs both.

132. **Socket.IO Redis startup failure and shared-network review (2026-10-21):** `docker-compose.yml` sets `NODE_ENV=production`; if `SOCKET_IO_MULTI_INSTANCE` is blank, runtime config defaults it to `true`, and a blank `SOCKET_IO_REDIS_URL` throws before Redis connection is attempted. This repo's Compose currently attaches the backend only to external `db_net`; it must also join `redis_net` for that DNS route. The installed `@socket.io/redis-adapter@8.3.0` is instantiated without an app-specific key, so it uses the shared default `socket.io` pub/sub channel prefix; separate ACL usernames alone are not sufficient channel isolation. 139. **Coolify Socket.IO Redis channel NOPERM diagnosis (2026-10-21):** On Redis 7.4.11, owner reports `ACL DRYRUN` denied `PSUBSCRIBE "beauty-salon:#/#*"`, while literal `SUBSCRIBE` request/response channels and `PUNSUBSCRIBE` passed. Redis 7 ACL source checks a PSUBSCRIBE argument literally against channel ACL entries; a glob ACL such as `&beauty-salon:*` matches literal channels but not this pattern string. The Redis adapter uses `key#/#*` for its root-namespace pattern. Narrow fix: add exact `&beauty-salon:#/#*` without resetting the ACL user, then verify and save; do not widen to `&*`.
140. **Redis ACL PSUBSCRIBE guidance corrected (2026-10-21):** Updated `.env.example` and `IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` to document the exact root-namespace PSUBSCRIBE rule and narrowly scoped request/response channel patterns. 176. **API-02 soak sample: first CI attempt exposed HTTP listener reset (2026-10-22):** CI run `39775340983` for commit `f85b6a5` passed repository, frontend and mobile jobs but the DB-backed HTTP suite failed in the new soak test with `ECONNRESET 127.0.0.1` before its 60-second window completed. 209. **SEC-04/PRIV-01 UX review after owner feedback (2026-10-23):** User asked how consent is granted, how a client learns that a photo is used, how albums/photos are managed, and directed that only the account holder may set their avatar. Source review confirms consent is per eligible before/after image after a completed linked service: client opens Profile, reviews the policy and photo, checks the explicit opt-in, grants it; profile then shows active status/consent date and revoke. Grant messaging exists, but there is no separate notification or link to the public placement. The profile consent card is shown only to `CLIENT`; if no eligible photos exist it shows an empty state. The admin Gallery screen has a `Nova Kategorija` category CRUD flow for `OWNER`/`SYSTEM_ADMIN`, but deliberately has no image-add picker/upload; public `/galerija` combines the consented before/after feed with existing category entries. The admin form reports only a generic error, so a failed category request's reason is not surfaced. Web Profile has a self-avatar upload control, but persists the selected path only when the profile form is saved; mobile Edit Profile uploads and persists immediately. Admin Users UI still exposes avatar upload on create/edit, and backend `UsersService.update` allows admin/owner avatar mutation, conflicting with the new self-only direction. Generic avatar uploads are private and owner-bound; an admin-uploaded file may not be readable by the target client. Prior policy kept avatars private; user now indicates self-upload implies approval, but public visibility scope is not specified. Treat self-upload as permission for own profile-avatar use only unless user explicitly approves public website exposure; do not infer gallery/social/ad consent. The reported UI behaviour was not runtime-reproduced; no source change, deployment, DB or upload was executed in this step. The checkout has broad pre-existing dirty changes; nothing was staged, committed or pushed. Next action: clarify album model (automatic feed vs admin category assignment limited to actively consented items) and avatar visibility; then, if approved, implement the self-only server-side avatar rule and reliable profile flow without staging unrelated dirty paths.

210. **SEC-04/PRIV-01 hybrid gallery + public-avatar policy approved (2026-10-23):** Owner confirms a hybrid: one category may be configured to auto-include eligible consented before/after pairs; only one auto category may be enabled, with a clear warning/rejection on a second. Other categories are manual and may accept direct admin/owner uploads for non-client salon/product media plus active-consent client photos selected from a restricted picker. No staff override or arbitrary unconsented client photos. Users may independently set whether their own avatar is publicly visible; default private and self-managed. Treat this as consent only for public avatar/profile use—not gallery, ads or social channels. Keep revocation immediate, ensure a public before/after pair requires active consent on both sides, and show clients whether consent is active vs actually published. Implementation must use backend and DB constraints, not UI-only controls; preserve broad existing dirty worktree and do not access production/shared data or deploy.

222. **Avatar visibility bug report and preliminary source trace (2026-10-23):** The owner reports that replacing an avatar, enabling the public-visibility checkbox and saving leaves it invisible both to the owner and publicly. The first pass saw `Profile.tsx` and shell layouts pass `private/avatars/...webp` through `getUploadUrl()` and tentatively attributed the symptom to a protected image URL lacking Bearer auth. That was only a hypothesis and did not yet account for the shared `AvatarImage` wrapper; Step 223 records the corrective trace. The same first pass observed that `readPrivateFile()` labels generated WebP variants with the original upload MIME type; the public avatar endpoint emits `image/webp`. Review/stylist serializers expose `/api/users/{id}/avatar` only when `isAvatarPublic` is true; the homepage renders a profile avatar only beside a randomly featured review, and no standalone public profile view was identified. No source changes or avatar-specific tests had been made at this point. Initial branch state was clean at local/remote `0f92dfb`; no production/shared data, browser/CORS, migration, PR, merge or deployment was used. The final status of docs-only CI run `39955204710` remains unknown.

223. **Avatar serving trace correction and persistence/public-surface review (2026-10-23):** Followed `AvatarImage` through `frontend/src/components/ui/avatar.tsx` into `useMediaImageSource`; protected `/api/uploads/content` URLs are fetched with the Axios `api` client and its Bearer-token interceptor, then rendered from a Blob object URL. Therefore the preliminary missing-Bearer explanation in Step 222 is rejected. A concrete, owner-view-serving defect is confirmed in source: `UploadsService.writeImageVariants()` stores `thumb/small/medium.webp`, while `readPrivateFile()` returns `upload.mimeType` for every selected variant; for a JPEG/PNG upload the protected response labels WebP bytes with the original JPEG/PNG MIME. The public avatar reader already returns the correct `image/webp`. Upload flow traces to private `saveAvatar()` → owner-validated `setOwnAvatar()` → `User.avatar`; visibility is persisted by the separate authenticated `PATCH /api/users/profile/avatar-visibility` and loaded by `GET /api/users/profile`. No defect in these persistence checks was established by static inspection. Public review/stylist serializers route opted-in avatars to `/api/users/{id}/avatar`; public endpoint checks `isAvatarPublic` and an owner-bound variant. The public homepage only renders the randomly featured review's avatar (if any); there is no standalone client public-profile view in the searched callsites, so that route's existence does not guarantee a given client's avatar appears on every public visit. Mobile's `MediaImage` separately attaches Bearer auth for protected files. Step 224 records the focused fix and tests. The final status of docs-only CI run `39955204710` remains unknown.

229. **Post-redeploy avatar report; native multipart defect confirmed and corrected (2026-10-23):** The owner now reports that after logging out/in, selecting a replacement avatar still leaves only initials; the client platform is not specified. The browser Profile uses initials fallback, but static tracing of its POST/state update/private Blob-fetch chain found no additional proven fault after Step 224; deployment/browser behavior cannot be inferred without the requests' statuses and response `Content-Type`. A separate, confirmed native-mobile issue was found in `mobile/src/services/api.ts`: its Axios instance declares global `Content-Type: application/json` and the React Native FormData path had no removal. Axios 1.13's installed `transformRequest` then serialized a native-style FormData request as JSON (reproduced as `{"file":{}}` with the app's Axios config), so Multer would receive no avatar file. The interceptor now removes JSON Content-Type when request data is FormData, preserving the multipart body and bearer token; the mobile picker now uses its actual `mimeType`/filename (or extension-derived fallback) instead of labelling every asset JPEG. Added `mobile/src/services/api-multipart.test.cjs`, npm script and CI step. Local mobile multipart test passes 1/1, socket recovery 6/6, and mobile typecheck passes; repo hygiene, 560 Markdown link checks and diff checks pass. Targeted Prettier with single quotes passes for the API client and new test; no broad reformat was applied to the pre-existing single-quote `EditProfileScreen`/workflow files. The web issue remains unresolved absent browser Network evidence. No production/browser/CORS test, PR, merge or deployment.

233. **Web avatar stream serialization defect isolated and corrected in source (2026-10-23):** Owner confirmed browser/web and supplied the successful avatar POST response (avatar path, public visibility true) plus a following body containing `StreamableFile` internals (`options`, `logger`, `stream`) and a 7,614-byte buffer starting with `RIFF`. Added unit regressions and enabled the real global interceptor in the disposable-PostgreSQL HTTP harness; existing avatar checks assert `image/webp` content type and raw binary body, so CI can catch the former JSON serialization. 241. **BIZ-02 read-only business-rule review (2026-10-23):** BIZ-01 is complete on this branch; BIZ-02 is now active and no BIZ-02 source/schema/docs implementation has yet been made. Source review confirmed slot semantics: `CONFIRMED`/`ATTENDANCE_CONFIRMED`/`IN_PROGRESS` occupy a named eligible stylist; `PENDING`/`STYLIST_REVIEW` holds consume one unit of capacity, with an active stylist lock assigning that hold to its locked stylist; canceled statuses do not consume capacity. Availability already permits simultaneous appointments up to eligible stylist capacity, uses the sum of selected service durations (the salon pause affects generated slot cadence, not the duration within a multi-service appointment), and separately enforces client same-day overlap/duplicate-service rules. BIZ-02 implementation will retain these rules and use a coarse PostgreSQL transaction-scoped salon-date advisory lock so concurrent standard/direct bookings and serialized reschedule rechecks cannot exceed the existing capacity; distinct stylists on the same date/time remain allowed up to calculated capacity. This is not a blind unique `(date,startTime)` rule. Broader lifecycle/race coverage remains distinct under BIZ-07. Idempotency keys will be carried by first-party web/mobile client create requests and stored with a DB unique constraint; stale/replayed requests must return the same reservation before re-running mutable availability checks. A transactional reservation-created outbox will replace creation-path side effects; immediate best-effort dispatch stays post-commit, with automated worker/retry operations remaining scheduled for the later queue phase as the plan specifies. Only synthetic/disposable CI resources may be used; no production/shared DB, actual Redis, browser/CORS, deployment, PR, merge or session close.

The DB-backed HTTP harness now uses those real booking services (mocking SMTP/push/Socket.IO) and provisions 20 synthetic clients; cleanup covers reservations/outbox and the extra stylist profile. New cases exercise same-key replay/no duplicate graph/outbox, one-winner concurrency at one stylist capacity, distinct-stylist direct bookings vs same-stylist overlap, cancellation capacity release, accepted reschedule conflict prevention, and rollback of reservation/loyalty/marker/outbox on a synthetic outbox insert failure. A unit guard also rejects outbox dispatch before transaction completion. 257. **BIZ-02 root-cause CI evidence (2026-10-24; run `40072736246`, head `82ffce5`):** Run je prošao Prisma generate/schema/migration, backend build, sanitizer/unit, frontend/mobile i repo checks. DB-backed HTTP suite i dalje pada u prvom client create; redacted logger now reveals `P2010` from `$queryRaw` with PostgreSQL message `Failed to deserialize column of type 'void'`. Ovo potvrđuje da se `void` rezultat `pg_advisory_xact_lock` ne može direktno vratiti kroz Prisma `$queryRaw`; problem je dekodiranje rezultata, ne lock contention ili availability greška. Lokalno implementirano: `acquireLock` sada radi `SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(...) IS NULL AS acquired`, tako da Prisma vraća boolean umjesto `void`, uz isti lock poziv unutar iste transakcije. Targeted formatting/backend generated-client i disposable CI još nisu rerun-ovani; BIZ-02 nije source-verified. Bez production/shared DB ili deploymenta.

Read-only review found concrete race windows in reschedule timeout (`update` by ID after stale read, then cancellation counter/notification), delay/appointment/daily-log reminders (send before null marker update), and direct-booking daily-log seed (transaction rechecks an existing row but `reservationId` has no unique constraint). KUF get-or-create is find-then-create under a unique period key, so it cannot duplicate the book row but a competing create can fail; no KUF symptom behavior was changed. Existing protections include conditional update/count checks in attendance/reorder/inbox paths, a leased atomic outbox claim with documented at-least-once external delivery, and KIF unique upsert/conditional book locking. These are source-control-flow findings, not observed incidents; current replica count, deployment config and two-instance DB behavior remain unknown. Owner decision #6 explicitly requests job replica count/topology. OPS-01 is selected as the next candidate, but runtime single-scheduler/advisory-lock work is held until the owner confirms current and intended replica topology; no production/shared resource was accessed. OPS-01 audit-doc commit `be2ccdb` passed all four jobs in CI run `40225800170` (backend/disposable-PostgreSQL CI, frontend, mobile and repository checks); preceding OPS-03 documentation commit `079a40a` also passed all four in `40225116909`. No application code, schema, user DB or deployment was changed in this phase.

29. **OPS-01 single-replica overlap/CAS source mitigation (2026-10-25; CI run `40227330516` passed):** Commit `e3bb254` implements the owner-selected single-scheduler strategy. The owner reports one current and intended backend replica; this remains user-reported and was not verified from deployment config. All four active `@Cron` entrypoints set Nest Schedule `waitForCompletion: true`, so overlapping invocations of the same callback in that process are skipped; this is not a distributed lock and missed ticks are not queued. `ReservationCronService.processPendingRescheduleTimeouts` now uses `updateMany` constrained by ID, observed status, `rescheduleStatus=PENDING` and proposed times; cancellation count and stylist notification run only when exactly one row changes. 30. **Frontend Docker build context failure and fix (2026-10-25; CI run `40229785202` passed all four jobs):** The user supplied TypeScript `TS2307` errors for imports of `shared/business-date.mjs` during frontend image build. Source inspection confirmed that `docker-compose.yml` and `docker-compose copy.yml` used `./frontend` as build context, excluding the repository-root `shared/` module. Prior frontend CI checked `npm run build` from the full checkout and therefore did not exercise that Docker boundary. Commit `c9a6171` switches both Compose configs to repository-root context with `frontend/Dockerfile`; the Dockerfile installs frontend dependencies, copies `frontend/` and `shared/` into matching paths, then builds. Compose validation asserts the primary service context and Dockerfile, and the frontend CI job successfully built the image with synthetic `.invalid` API URLs. Local `npm run build` passes (4,835 modules), socket recovery passes 6/6; Docker CLI is unavailable locally. This is synthetic image-build evidence only: no image push, deployment, production/shared resource access or independent Coolify service-configuration check occurred. If Coolify bypasses repository Compose, configure the frontend build context as the repository root and Dockerfile as `frontend/Dockerfile`.

31. **Backend upload permission and rate-limit Redis symptoms (2026-10-25; user-reported, no runtime access):** The supplied `POST /api/users/profile/avatar` stack ends at `EPERM chmod /usr/app/uploads/private`. Source/Docker review shows backend UID/GID `10001:10001`, private storage mode `0700`, and Compose bind mount `/mnt/shared-app-files/beauty-salon-v2:/usr/app/uploads`; the host mount overlays the ownership prepared in the image. The most likely cause is host `private/` ownership/ACL or a filesystem that disallows chmod; actual host mode was not inspected. The `mkdir/chmod` failure occurs before bytes and Upload DB row are written, so the failed requests did not create new avatar files/records. Do not remove private-mode enforcement or use `0777`; runbook Step “Runtime follow-up: upload permissions and HTTP rate-limit Redis” contains operator-only inspection and narrowly scoped remediation for UID 10001. Existing avatars remain private unless the owner opts into `isAvatarPublic`; same-user private reads use the authenticated content endpoint. The warning `HTTP_RATE_LIMIT_REDIS_URL is not configured` means HTTP quotas fail open; it is a separate missing runtime setting, not evidence that Socket.IO Redis failed. Coolify must receive the dedicated secret and `HTTP_RATE_LIMIT_PREFIX=beauty-salon-v2:http-rate-limit:production`; no credential was requested or recorded. The sampled socket connect/disconnect logs include no reason and do not establish a backend error. No backend source change, host filesystem/DB/Redis access, migration, deployment or image push was performed.

**KUF-01 targeted expense-save validation fix (2026-10-26; implementation committed, local targeted test passes, CI rerun pending):** Variable-category expense `notes` stores the serialized line-item array and was capped by both create/update DTOs at 2,000 characters, causing larger invoices to fail validation before save. The DTO payload cap is now 20,000 characters; the actual human-entered note remains limited to 2,000 characters through frontend `maxLength` and category-aware backend validation of plain/fixed notes or embedded `userNotes`. Added a focused Jest spec for >2,000-character create/update payload acceptance, line-item sum/template validation, the 20,000-character ceiling and the preserved user-note cap. No schema/migration change is required (`Expense.notes` is PostgreSQL `TEXT`). Commit `5a69cd3` was pushed to `arena/657dba19-beauty-salon-v2`. Initial CI run `40370657964` passed mobile, repository, frontend build, backend build/schema/migrations on disposable PostgreSQL and HTTP tests; backend unit tests failed before assertions because the new DTO spec omitted `reflect-metadata`. Added the import; after `npm ci --ignore-scripts`, the focused Jest suite passes 1 suite/4 tests. Corrective commit `c81ef61` is pushed; CI run `40371832221` passed all four jobs, including the full backend unit suite and disposable-PostgreSQL checks. The separate KUF test runs only synthetic validation; no production/shared database was used. The frontend build passed in CI; the local build was not run because `tsc` was initially absent. 88. **AUTH-02 partial — one-time generated credentials and backend forced-change enforcement (2026-10-27):** Removed the shared initial-password constant. Admin-created accounts without a supplied password now get a 192-bit cryptographically random, base64url credential returned only from the protected create response; readback excludes auth hashes and verification tokens. Admin UI has no default-password fallback and clears credential state when its one-time dialog closes. `mustChangePassword` is loaded from the database and enforced by the global HTTP JWT guard: only profile bootstrap and password-change routes are allowed; authenticated Socket.IO handshakes for forced-change accounts are denied. Added route-matrix, user-creation, socket and frontend tests; all four CI jobs passed run `40504091497`. Next: AUTH-01 throttling/verification abuse controls and SEC-08 runtime XSS acceptance, following Phase 0E; deployment/owner gates remain separate.


89. **AUTH-01 partial — application auth throttles and shorter email-code lifetime (2026-10-27):** Added multi-policy support to the shared Redis HTTP rate-limit interceptor. Login is limited to 30/IP/min and 10/normalized account/15 min; registration to 10/IP/hour; code verification to 10/IP/15 min; resend to 3/user/hour and 10/IP/hour. Rate-limit response headers represent the tightest active policy. Inactive-account login errors now match invalid credentials. New six-digit email-verification codes expire after 15 minutes and are stored as domain-separated HMAC-SHA256 digests keyed by `JWT_SECRET`; malformed input is rejected before DB lookup and successful codes are consumed. Existing plaintext pending codes will require resend after rollout. Default notification/settings copy matches; saved custom templates were not inspected or edited. Added interceptor/metadata, expiry, digest lookup and consumption tests. CI run `40508454668` passed backend build/unit/DB-backed suite, frontend build/tests, mobile TypeScript and repository jobs. Local frontend suite passed 92 tests, production build passed with existing >500 kB advisory; strict locale parity 1,598/1,598 and repo hygiene/docs links/contrast checks passed. Backend Jest was not run locally (backend tools unavailable); TypeScript syntax transpilation only was run locally. **Residual:** Redis failure deliberately fails open; actual Redis/proxy config is not verified; six-digit verification lacks per-challenge attempt counter; register is IP-only due multipart parsing; chosen limits need owner review for lockout/DoS trade-offs; password reset, account recovery and saved custom-template audit remain open. No production DB or deployment was accessed. Implementation commit `a576151`; next validate runtime/edge limiter and close remaining AUTH-01 decisions, then proceed Phase 0E residuals and next audit phases.

Do not apply either suggestion blindly: the first is an SDK downgrade, while Router58's dependencies/peer set is SDK58. Registry metadata shows current Expo 58.0.20 uses `@expo/cli` 58.1.16, which still declares Zod `^3.25.76`; GHSA-mrhx-c7xc-m3w5 affects Zod `<4.6.6`, so an SDK bump alone does not clear the CLI/Zod risk. Resolving this needs an upstream compatible Expo CLI/Zod patch or an explicitly reviewed Zod-major override, plus coordinated SDK58/Expo Router migration and native acceptance. `npx expo install --check` could not reach Expo's remote endpoint (TLS socket disconnected); this made no changes. No Expo/React Native/Expo Router package was upgraded in this step; frontend Router patch CI run `40561532954` is green.

106. **SCM-01 SDK57 Expo Router URI/query-string compatibility patch (2026-10-27):** The last three mobile production advisories were `expo-router`/`query-string`/`decode-uri-component`. Registry metadata identifies `query-string@9.5.1` (patched query-string line, Node >=18) and its `decode-uri-component@0.5.1` (patched >=0.5.0); both remain scoped under the existing `expo-router@57.0.15`, with no Expo/RN/Router SDK major change. A plain lock override was rejected after its first Node smoke failed: Expo Router's four compiled SDK57 modules use `__importStar(require('query-string'))`, but query-string9 is ESM-default-exported and the result had no `.parse`. Added a fail-closed postinstall adapter (`mobile/scripts/patch-expo-router-query-string.js`) that only handles Expo Router57 and replaces those four CJS interop imports with `module.default || module`; source shape/version changes throw instead of silently skipping. Repeated-key/encoded-space parse and stringify regression passes; the Node support floor is already met by React Native 0.86 (`^20.19.4 || ^22.13.0 || ^24.3.0 || >=25`). Clean `npm ci` runs both existing asset patch and the guarded Router shim. Expo CLI/Zod schema tests and mobile typecheck/multipart pass. `npx expo export` successfully bundled iOS (2,740 modules) and Android (2,827 modules) to `/tmp`; this is Metro JS bundling only, not a native Xcode/Gradle/device build.
